# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Brazil 1970 national programmes.
COLDWAR_BRA_event_apply_officer_council_option_0 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_chosen_officer_council
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_chosen_officer_council
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_officer_council_option_0
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_officer_council_option_0
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_officer_council_option_1
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_officer_council_option_0
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_event_apply_party_reform_option_0 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_chosen_party_reform
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_chosen_party_reform
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_party_reform_option_0
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_party_reform_option_0
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_party_reform_option_1
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_party_reform_option_0
		COLDWAR_BRA_1970_open_party_reform = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_democratic_pluralism
		enable_elections_with_no_leader_change = yes
		COLDWAR_BRA_open_organisation_stages = yes
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_event_apply_party_reform_option_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_chosen_party_reform
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_chosen_party_reform
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_party_reform_option_1
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_party_reform_option_0
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_party_reform_option_1
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_party_reform_option_1
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = the_military
			}
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 7 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 21 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 }
					}
				}
				divide_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 2 }
			}
			change_the_military_opinion = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_BRA_1970_open_party_reform = yes
		COLDWAR_BRA_open_organisation_stages = yes
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_event_apply_debt_crisis_option_0 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_chosen_debt_crisis
			}
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_chosen_debt_crisis
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_debt_crisis_option_0
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_debt_crisis_option_0
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_debt_crisis_option_1
		add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_BRA_policy_event_debt_crisis_option_0 days = 365 }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.003 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_military_0 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_0
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_military_in_power = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_0
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_military_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_1
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_military_in_power = yes
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_military_0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_military_2
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_1
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = the_military
			}
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 8 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 7 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 21 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 }
					}
				}
				divide_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 2 }
			}
			change_the_military_opinion = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_military_2 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_2
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_military_in_power = yes
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_military_0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_military_1
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_2
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0015 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_ai5
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = the_military
			}
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -8 }
			change_the_military_opinion = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_military_3 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_3
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_military_in_power = yes
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_military_1
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_military_2
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_3
		COLDWAR_BRA_fire_officer_council = yes
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_military_4 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_4
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_military_in_power = yes
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_military_0
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_4
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.003 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = COLDWAR_BRA_military_4_research_1
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = CAT_electronics
		}
		army_experience = 25
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_military_5 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_5
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_military_in_power = yes
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			owns_state = 884
			controls_state = 884
			has_full_control_of_state = 884
			884 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@arms_factory value = 34 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_military_3
			}
		}
		BRA = {
			884 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				BRA = {
					884 = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 0
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_military_5
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0065 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			884 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = arms_factory
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
		}
		else = {
			BRA = {
				884 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_military_9 = {

		limit = {

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_arena_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_arena_1
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_arena_in_power = yes
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			owns_state = 887
			controls_state = 887
			has_full_control_of_state = 887
			887 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_arena_0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_arena_2
			}
		}
		BRA = {
			887 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				BRA = {
					887 = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = offices
							size > 0
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_arena_1
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.004 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			887 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
		}
		else = {
			BRA = {
				887 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_ptb_2
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_in_power = yes
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_0
			}
			NOT = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_1
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_ptb_2
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = labour_unions
			}
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 15 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 7 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 21 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 }
					}
				}
				divide_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 2 }
			}
			change_labour_unions_opinion = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_ptb_3 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_ptb_3
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_in_power = yes
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			owns_state = 882
			controls_state = 882
			has_full_control_of_state = 882
			882 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_1
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_2
			}
		}
		BRA = {
			882 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				BRA = {
					882 = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = offices
							size > 0
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_ptb_3
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0025 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			882 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
		}
		else = {
			BRA = {
				882 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_ptb_5 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_ptb_5
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_in_power = yes
			has_political_power > 24
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			owns_state = 883
			controls_state = 883
			has_full_control_of_state = 883
			883 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_0
			}
		}
		BRA = {
			883 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				BRA = {
					883 = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = offices
							size > 0
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_ptb_5
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.002 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0015 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			883 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			add_political_power = -25
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
		}
		else = {
			BRA = {
				883 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_pcb_3 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_pcb_3
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_pcb_in_power = yes
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			owns_state = 883
			controls_state = 883
			has_full_control_of_state = 883
			883 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_pcb_1
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_pcb_2
			}
		}
		BRA = {
			883 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				BRA = {
					883 = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = offices
							size > 0
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_pcb_3
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.004 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			883 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
		}
		else = {
			BRA = {
				883 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_populist_3 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_populist_3
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_populist_in_power = yes
			has_political_power > 29
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			owns_state = 883
			controls_state = 883
			has_full_control_of_state = 883
			883 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_populist_1
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_populist_2
			}
		}
		BRA = {
			883 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				BRA = {
					883 = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = offices
							size > 0
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_populist_3
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.003 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			883 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			add_political_power = -30
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
		}
		else = {
			BRA = {
				883 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		if = {
			limit = {

				limit = {
					OR = {
						western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes

						check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 21 }
						check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 }
					}
				}
				divide_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 2 }
			}

		}

	}

			}

			}

			}

		}
		else = {

				891 = {

				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_imbel = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_imbel
			}
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			owns_state = 884
			controls_state = 884
			has_full_control_of_state = 884
			884 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@arms_factory value = 34 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_territorial
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_professional
			}
		}
		BRA = {
			884 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				BRA = {
					884 = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = arms_factory
							size > 0
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_imbel
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0065 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			884 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = arms_factory
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
		}
		else = {
			BRA = {
				884 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_regional_secretariat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_regional_secretariat
			}
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			owns_state = 885
			controls_state = 885
			has_full_control_of_state = 885
			885 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@offices value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_uruguay
			}
		}
		BRA = {
			885 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				BRA = {
					885 = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = offices
							size > 0
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_regional_secretariat
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0025 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			885 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
		}
		else = {
			BRA = {
				885 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_complete_pds_private_suppliers = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_pds_private_suppliers
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_pds_in_power = yes
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			owns_state = 882
			controls_state = 882
			has_full_control_of_state = 882
			882 = {
				check_variable = { var = building_level@industrial_complex value = 49 compare = less_than_or_equals }
			}
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_pds_enterprise_accounts
			}
			NOT = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_pds_strategic_holdings
			}
		}
		BRA = {
			882 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				BRA = {
					882 = {
						free_building_slots = {
							building = industrial_complex
							size > 0
							include_locked = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_pds_private_suppliers
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0075 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			882 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = COLDWAR_BRA_pds_private_suppliers_research_2
				bonus = 0.25
				uses = 1
				category = CAT_industry
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
		}
		else = {
			BRA = {
				882 = {
					add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = -1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_decision_apply_service_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_unlock_service_mission
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_decision_service_mission
			}
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			COLDWAR_BRA_military_in_power = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_decision_service_mission
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.002 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		army_experience = 30
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_decision_apply_uranium_study = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_unlock_uranium_study
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_decision_uranium_study
			}
			check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			any_owned_state = {
				is_controlled_by = BRA
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = imp_uranium_deposit }
				has_variable = imp_uranium_per_level
				imp_uranium_investment_one_level_available = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_decision_uranium_study
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total } multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0015 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = COLDWAR_BRA_decision_uranium_study_research_1
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = CAT_nuclear_reactors
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes }
		COLDWAR_BRA_init_politics = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_initialized }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_ai5
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_miracle
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_wage_squeeze
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_skills
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_land_inequality
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_urban_pressure
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_oil_exposure
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_BRA_amazon_pressure
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_initialized
			COLDWAR_BRA_reconcile_politics = yes
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_programmes = yes
			COLDWAR_BRA_sync_agreements = yes
		}
	}
}

		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_late_2
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_ptb_late_2
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_complete_ptb_late_2 = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_late_3
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_ptb_late_3
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_complete_ptb_late_3 = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_BRA_ptb_late_4
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_earned_ptb_late_4
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_complete_ptb_late_4 = yes
		}
		if = {

				NOT = {

COLDWAR_BRA_political_pressure_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_sent_oil_shock
				}
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = imp_oil_prices_high }
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_fire_oil_shock = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_sent_debt_crisis
				}
				date > 1981.12.31
				check_variable = { debt_ratio > 0.25 }
				check_variable = { interest_rate > 6 }
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_fire_debt_crisis = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_sent_inflation_settlement
				}
				inflation_rate_higher_than_3 = yes
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_fire_inflation_settlement = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_event_sent_us_rights
				}
				date > 1976.12.31
				has_idea = COLDWAR_BRA_us_training_BRA
			}
			COLDWAR_BRA_fire_us_rights = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_BRA_open_organisation_stages = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_BRA_campaign = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_party_reform_open
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_labourist_restoration_open
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_labourist_reorganization_open
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_democratic_pluralism
						date > 1984.12.31
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_communist_legalization_open
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_party_pds_registered
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_pds_split_authorized
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_democratic_pluralism
						date > 1987.12.31
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_party_pmdb_registered
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_pmdb_split_authorized
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					date > 1988.12.31
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_prn_formation_open
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_BRA_1970_party_identity_3 = 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_BRA_1970_party_identity_3 = 1 }
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_party_historic_ptb_restored
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_pcb_alternative_accession
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { ruling_party = 19 }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_BRA_1970_pcdob_alternative_accession
		}
	}
}
